 420+ 6B02
 421+ 6B02                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_layout.asm"
# file opened: p127_layout.asm
   1++6B02              ; ============================================================================
   2++6B02              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6B02              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6B02              ;
   5++6B02              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6B02              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6B02              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6B02              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6B02              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6B02              ; ============================================================================
  11++6B02              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6B02              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6B02              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6B02              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6B02              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6B02              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6B02
  18++6B02              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6B02                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6B02 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6B05 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6B08 B7                   or      a
  23++6B09 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6B0B C2 4A 6B             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6B0E
  26++6B0E                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6B0E 21 38 7D             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6B11 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6B14 B7                   or      a
  30++6B15 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6B17 DA 4A 6B             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6B1A CA 4A 6B             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6B1D 22 64 6B             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6B20 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6B21 FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6B23 D2 4A 6B             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6B26
  38++6B26 21 38 7D             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6B29 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6B2C B7                   or      a
  41++6B2D ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6B2F DA 38 6B             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6B32 CA 38 6B             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6B35 C3 4A 6B             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6B38              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6B38                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6B38 3A 47 72             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6B3B FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6B3D C2 4A 6B             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6B40 3A 44 72             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6B43 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6B45 C2 4A 6B             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6B48 B7                   or      a
  54++6B49 C9                   ret
  55++6B4A
  56++6B4A              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6B4A 37                   scf
  58++6B4B C9                   ret
  59++6B4C
  60++6B4C              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6B4C              p127_layout_report:
  62++6B4C 21 66 6B             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size
  63++6B4F CD 34 62             call    p127_puts
  64++6B52 3A 65 6B             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size+1)
  65++6B55 CD 50 62             call    p127_put_hex8
  66++6B58 3A 64 6B             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size)
  67++6B5B CD 50 62             call    p127_put_hex8
  68++6B5E 21 82 6B             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size_tail
  69++6B61 C3 34 62             jp      p127_puts
  70++6B64
  71++6B64              p127_kernel_size:
  72++6B64 00 00                dw      0
  73++6B66              p127_msg_kernel_size:
  74++6B66 5B 50 31 32          db      "[P127] KERNEL IMAGE SIZE=0x",0
  74++6B6A 37 5D 20 4B

   1++6B9E              ; ============================================================================
   2++6B9E              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6B9E              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6B9E              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6B9E              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6B9E              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6B9E              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6B9E              ; ============================================================================
   9++6B9E              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6B9E              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6B9E              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6B9E
  13++6B9E              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6B9E              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6B9E              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6B9E              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6B9E              p127_resident_start:
  18++6B9E
  19++6B9E              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6B9E              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6B9E              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6B9E              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6B9E              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6B9E              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6B9E
  26++6B9E              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6B9E              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6B9E              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6B9E              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6B9E              p127_resident_validate:

 348++6DCF              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 349++6DCF              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++6DCF              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++6DCF              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++6DCF              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++6DCF              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++6DCF              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++6DCF              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.
 356++6DCF              p127_resident_trampoline_preflight:
 357++6DCF F3                   di
 358++6DD0                      ; Linked code and data must remain wholly inside the fixed W1 window.
 359++6DD0 21 9E 6B             ld      hl,p127_resident_start
 360++6DD3 11 00 60             ld      de,06000h
 361++6DD6 B7                   or      a
 362++6DD7 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 363++6DD9 DA 1C 6E             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 364++6DDC
 365++6DDC 21 1E 6E             ld      hl,p127_resident_end
 366++6DDF 11 00 80             ld      de,08000h
 367++6DE2 B7                   or      a
 368++6DE3 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 369++6DE5 D2 1C 6E             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 370++6DE8
 371++6DE8                      ; Representative mutable state must be within the resident module.
 372++6DE8 21 C9 6D             ld      hl,p127_w3_state
 373++6DEB 11 9E 6B             ld      de,p127_resident_start
 374++6DEE B7                   or      a
 375++6DEF ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 376++6DF1 DA 1C 6E             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 377++6DF4
 378++6DF4 21 C9 6D             ld      hl,p127_w3_state
 379++6DF7 11 1E 6E             ld      de,p127_resident_end
 380++6DFA B7                   or      a
 381++6DFB ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 382++6DFD D2 1C 6E             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 383++6E00
 384++6E00                      ; CALL has already pushed its return address. The live stack must
 385++6E00                      ; still be within W2, independent of W3's mapping.
 386++6E00 21 00 00             ld      hl,0

   1++6E1E              ; ============================================================================
   2++6E1E              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++6E1E              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++6E1E              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++6E1E              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++6E1E              ; ============================================================================
   7++6E1E              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++6E1E              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++6E1E              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++6E1E              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++6E1E              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++6E1E              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++6E1E
  14++6E1E              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++6E1E              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++6E1E              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++6E1E              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++6E1E              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++6E1E              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++6E1E              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++6E1E              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++6E1E              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++6E1E              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++6E1E              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++6E1E
  26++6E1E              p127_irq_init:
  27++6E1E F3                   di
  28++6E1F 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  29++6E21 32 00 71             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  30++6E24 AF                   xor     a
  31++6E25 32 01 71             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++6E28 32 02 71             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  33++6E2B 32 03 71             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  34++6E2E B7                   or      a
  35++6E2F C9                   ret
  36++6E30

  38++6E30              p127_irq_validate:

   1++7104              ; ============================================================================
   2++7104              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++7104              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4++7104              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++7104              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++7104              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++7104              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++7104              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++7104              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++7104              ; ============================================================================
  11++7104              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++7104              P127_MEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  13++7104              P127_MEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3

   1++7282              ; ============================================================================
   2++7282              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++7282              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++7282              ; ABI:
   5++7282              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++7282              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++7282              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++7282              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++7282              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++7282              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.
  11++7282              ; ============================================================================
  12++7282              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  13++7282              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  14++7282              P127_RANGE_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  15++7282              P127_RANGE_LIMIT         EQU 0100000h
  16++7282
  17++7282              p127_range_validate:
  18++7282                      ; Add the unsigned 32-bit length to the unsigned start.
  19++7282 21 90 73             ld      hl,p127_range_start
  20++7285 11 94 73             ld      de,p127_range_length
  21++7288 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  22++7289 47                   ld      b,a
  23++728A 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  24++728B 80                   add     a,b
  25++728C 32 98 73             ld      (p127_range_end),a
  26++728F 23                   inc     hl
  27++7290 13                   inc     de
  28++7291 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  29++7292 47                   ld      b,a
  30++7293 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  31++7294 88                   adc     a,b
  32++7295 32 99 73             ld      (p127_range_end+1),a
  33++7298 23                   inc     hl
  34++7299 13                   inc     de
  35++729A 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  36++729B 47                   ld      b,a
  37++729C 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  38++729D 88                   adc     a,b
  39++729E 32 9A 73             ld      (p127_range_end+2),a

  42++72A3 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  43++72A4 47                   ld      b,a
  44++72A5 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  45++72A6 88                   adc     a,b
  46++72A7 32 9B 73             ld      (p127_range_end+3),a
  47++72AA D8                   ret     c                       ; unsigned 32-bit addition overflow
  48++72AB
  49++72AB                      ; End-exclusive address must be <= 0x00100000.
  50++72AB 3A 9B 73             ld      a,(p127_range_end+3)

   1++739C              ; ============================================================================
   2++739C              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++739C              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++739C              ;
   5++739C              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++739C              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++739C              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++739C              ;
   9++739C              ; API:
  10++739C              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++739C              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++739C              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++739C              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++739C              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++739C              ;
  16++739C              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++739C              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++739C              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++739C              ; ============================================================================
  20++739C              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++739C              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++739C              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++739C              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++739C              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++739C
  26++739C              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++739C              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++739C              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++739C              p127_arena_init:

   1++74FA              ; ============================================================================
   2++74FA              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++74FA              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++74FA              ;
   5++74FA              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++74FA              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++74FA              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++74FA              ;
   9++74FA              ; API:
  10++74FA              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++74FA              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++74FA              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++74FA              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++74FA              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++74FA              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++74FA              ; ============================================================================

  18++74FA              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++74FA              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0

 177++7AA7              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 178++7AA7              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++7AA7              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++7AA7              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++7AA7              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++7AA7              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++7AA7              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++7AA7              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).
 185++7AA7              p127_map_handoff_prepare:
 186++7AA7                      ; Keep the target in E, invalidate any older staged request, then
 187++7AA7                      ; validate the page/window and index the four-byte mask with DE=0:E.
 188++7AA7 5F                   ld      e,a
 189++7AA8 AF                   xor     a
 190++7AA9 32 68 7B             ld      (p127_map_pending_valid),a
 191++7AAC 78                   ld      a,b
 192++7AAD FE 40                cp      64

 194++7AB2 7B                   ld      a,e
 195++7AB3 FE 04                cp      P127_MAP_WINDOW_COUNT

 198++7ABA 21 64 7B             ld      hl,p127_map_window_mask

 200++7ABE 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 201++7ABF A1                   and     c
 202++7AC0 C2 6B 7B             jp      nz,p127_map_handoff_reject
 203++7AC3 7B                   ld      a,e
 204++7AC4 32 69 7B             ld      (p127_map_pending_window),a
 205++7AC7 78                   ld      a,b
 206++7AC8 32 6A 7B             ld      (p127_map_pending_page),a
 207++7ACB 3E 01                ld      a,1
 208++7ACD 32 68 7B             ld      (p127_map_pending_valid),a
 209++7AD0 B7                   or      a
 210++7AD1 C9                   ret
 211++7AD2
 212++7AD2              ; Cancel a staged request. No hardware state is modified.
 213++7AD2              p127_map_handoff_abort:
 214++7AD2 AF                   xor     a
 215++7AD3 32 68 7B             ld      (p127_map_pending_valid),a
 216++7AD6 C9                   ret

 310++7B75              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 311++7B75              ; Stage 2.14 — software-only transactional shadow-map update.
 312++7B75              ; Full four-window snapshot; commit changes RAM shadow state only.
 313++7B75              ; Rollback restores all four bytes, including after injected post-commit fault.
 314++7B75              ; No page-port OUT is performed by any routine in this block.
 315++7B75              ;
 316++7B75              ; txn_begin: A=window, B=new RAM page, C=protected-window mask.
 317++7B75              ; txn_commit: commits pending page into software shadow map.
 318++7B75              ; txn_rollback: restores complete snapshot and closes transaction.
 319++7B75              ; txn_accept: closes committed transaction while retaining new shadow map.
 320++7B75              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  10++7C9D
  11++7C9D              ; Current bootstrap stack is at #BFFF in W2/P2. Keep at least 128 bytes
  12++7C9D              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.
  13++7C9D              P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP    EQU 0B900h
  14++7C9D              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h
  15++7C9D              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP     EQU 0B880h
  16++7C9D              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO      EQU 0B7FEh
  17++7C9D              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++7C9D
  19++7C9D              ; IN: none.
  20++7C9D              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++7C9D              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++7C9D              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++7C9D              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++7C9D              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++7C9D              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++7C9D              p127_handoff_stack_probe:
  27++7C9D F3                   di
  28++7C9E F5                   push    af
  29++7C9F C5                   push    bc
  30++7CA0 D5                   push    de
  31++7CA1 E5                   push    hl
  32++7CA2 DD E5                push    ix
  33++7CA4 FD E5                push    iy
  34++7CA6 ED 73 35 7D          ld      (p127_handoff_saved_sp),sp
  35++7CAA
  36++7CAA                      ; Refuse if the live stack has crossed its declared low-water limit.
  37++7CAA 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
  38++7CAD 39                   add     hl,sp
  39++7CAE 11 00 B9             ld      de,P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP
  40++7CB1 B7                   or      a
  41++7CB2 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++7CB4 DA 05 7D             jp      c,p127_handoff_stack_refuse
  43++7CB7
  44++7CB7                      ; Canary bytes bracket the unused gap below the scratch stack.
  45++7CB7 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
  46++7CB9 32 FE B7             ld      (P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO),a
  47++7CBC 3E 5A                ld      a,05Ah
  48++7CBE 32 FF B7             ld      (P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI),a
  49++7CC1
  50++7CC1                      ; Switch only the software stack pointer. W2/P2 stays mapped.
  51++7CC1 31 80 B8             ld      sp,P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP
  52++7CC4 21 57 13             ld      hl,01357h
  53++7CC7 E5                   push    hl
  54++7CC8 11 68 24             ld      de,02468h
  55++7CCB D5                   push    de
  56++7CCC CD 1D 7D             call    p127_handoff_stack_inner
  57++7CCF DA FD 7C             jp      c,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  58++7CD2 D1                   pop     de
  59++7CD3 21 68 24             ld      hl,02468h
  60++7CD6 B7                   or      a
  61++7CD7 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  62++7CD9 C2 FD 7C             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  63++7CDC E1                   pop     hl
  64++7CDD 11 57 13             ld      de,01357h
  65++7CE0 B7                   or      a
  66++7CE1 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  67++7CE3 C2 FD 7C             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  68++7CE6
  69++7CE6 3A FE B7             ld      a,(P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO)
  70++7CE9 FE A5                cp      0A5h
  71++7CEB C2 FD 7C             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  72++7CEE 3A FF B7             ld      a,(P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI)
  73++7CF1 FE 5A                cp      05Ah
  74++7CF3 C2 FD 7C             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  75++7CF6
  76++7CF6 AF                   xor     a
  77++7CF7 32 37 7D             ld      (p127_handoff_result),a
  78++7CFA C3 0A 7D             jp      p127_handoff_stack_restore
  79++7CFD
  80++7CFD              p127_handoff_stack_fail:
  81++7CFD 3E 01                ld      a,1
  82++7CFF 32 37 7D             ld      (p127_handoff_result),a
  83++7D02 C3 0A 7D             jp      p127_handoff_stack_restore
  84++7D05
  85++7D05              p127_handoff_stack_refuse:
  86++7D05 3E 02                ld      a,2
  87++7D07 32 37 7D             ld      (p127_handoff_result),a
  88++7D0A
  89++7D0A              p127_handoff_stack_restore:
  90++7D0A                      ; Restore the exact saved SP before touching the caller's saved frame.
  91++7D0A ED 7B 35 7D          ld      sp,(p127_handoff_saved_sp)
  92++7D0E FD E1                pop     iy
  93++7D10 DD E1                pop     ix
  94++7D12 E1                   pop     hl
  95++7D13 D1                   pop     de
  96++7D14 C1                   pop     bc
  97++7D15 F1                   pop     af
  98++7D16 3A 37 7D             ld      a,(p127_handoff_result)
  99++7D19 B7                   or      a
 100++7D1A C8                   ret     z
 101++7D1B 37                   scf
 102++7D1C C9                   ret
 103++7D1D
 104++7D1D              ; Nested CALL/PUSH/POP test on the scratch stack; BC is preserved.
 105++7D1D              p127_handoff_stack_inner:
 106++7D1D C5                   push    bc
 107++7D1E 01 5A A5             ld      bc,0A55Ah
 108++7D21 C5                   push    bc
 109++7D22 C1                   pop     bc
 110++7D23 78                   ld      a,b
 111++7D24 FE A5                cp      0A5h
 112++7D26 C2 32 7D             jp      nz,p127_handoff_inner_fail
 113++7D29 79                   ld      a,c
 114++7D2A FE 5A                cp      05Ah
 115++7D2C C2 32 7D             jp      nz,p127_handoff_inner_fail
 116++7D2F C1                   pop     bc
 117++7D30 B7                   or      a
 118++7D31 C9                   ret
 119++7D32              p127_handoff_inner_fail:
 120++7D32 C1                   pop     bc
 121++7D33 37                   scf
 122++7D34 C9                   ret
 123++7D35
 124++7D35              p127_handoff_saved_sp:
 125++7D35 00 00                dw      0
 126++7D37              p127_handoff_result:
 127++7D37 00                   db      0
 128++7D38              p127_handoff_end:
 129++7D38
# file closed: p127_handoff.asm

  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A

  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77

 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so

  93  A3F2                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_physmem.asm"
# file opened: p127_physmem.asm
   1+ A3F2              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A3F2              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A3F2              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A3F2              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A3F2              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A3F2              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A3F2              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A3F2
  15+ A3F2              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A3F2              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A3F2              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A3F2              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A3F2              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A3F2              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A3F2              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:
  22+ A3F2 3E 53                ld      a,'S'
  23+ A3F4 CD A0 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
  24+ A3F7 AF                   xor     a
  25+ A3F8 32 63 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
  26+ A3FB 32 64 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a

 223+ A5DE
 224+ A5DE                      ; Stage 2.19: exhaust the allocator into a private page-ID list.
 225+ A5DE                      ; The first two pages are already listed. Each new allocation is
 226+ A5DE                      ; checked against all prior IDs before it is appended.
 227+ A5DE              p127_physmem_alloc_more:

 255+ A611              p127_physmem_alloc_complete:

 258+ A616                      ; Current map has exactly seven protected pages and 57 allocatable.
 259+ A616 3A 64 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)

 263+ A621 DA CD A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_reserve
 264+ A624
 265+ A624                      ; First sweep: fill every allocated physical page with a unique
 266+ A624                      ; address-derived pattern whose seed includes its physical page ID.
 267+ A624 3A 64 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 268+ A627 32 70 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 269+ A62A 21 71 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 270+ A62D              p127_physmem_full_fill_loop:
 271+ A62D 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 272+ A62E 23                   inc     hl
 273+ A62F 32 6F AB             ld      (p127_physmem_current_page),a
 274+ A632 3E 46                ld      a,'F'
 275+ A634 CD A6 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_page
 276+ A637 3A 6F AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 277+ A63A CD 26 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 278+ A63D DA B4 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
 279+ A640 3A 6F AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 280+ A643 EE 5A                xor     05Ah
 281+ A645 57                   ld      d,a
 282+ A646 E5                   push    hl
 283+ A647 CD 3A AB             call    p127_physmem_fill_pattern
 284+ A64A E1                   pop     hl
 285+ A64B 3A 70 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 286+ A64E 3D                   dec     a
 287+ A64F 32 70 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 288+ A652 20 D9                jr      nz,p127_physmem_full_fill_loop
 289+ A654
 290+ A654 3E 46                ld      a,'F'
 291+ A656 CD A0 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
 292+ A659
 293+ A659                      ; Reverse sweep verifies every page after all other pages have
 294+ A659                      ; already been written. This catches aliases and lost page contents.
 295+ A659 3A 64 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 296+ A65C 5F                   ld      e,a
 297+ A65D 16 00                ld      d,0
 298+ A65F 21 71 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 299+ A662 19                   add     hl,de
 300+ A663              p127_physmem_full_verify_loop:
 301+ A663 2B                   dec     hl
 302+ A664 7E                   ld      a,(hl)

 308+ A673 DA B4 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map

 311+ A67B 57                   ld      d,a

 313+ A67D CD 4C AB             call    p127_physmem_verify_pattern
 314+ A680 E1                   pop     hl
 315+ A681 DA BE A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_data
 316+ A684 3A 70 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 317+ A687                      ; Reinitialize the reverse counter on first entry.
 318+ A687 B7                   or      a
 319+ A688 20 03                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready
 320+ A68A 3A 64 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 321+ A68D              p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready:

 323+ A68E 32 70 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a

 335+ A6A5

 367+ A6D7

 370+ A6D9 CD A0 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
 371+ A6DC                      ; Always return W3 to P0 before releasing UI ownership.
 372+ A6DC 3A 67 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_mapped)
 373+ A6DF B7                   or      a
 374+ A6E0 28 07                jr      z,p127_physmem_cleanup_saved
 375+ A6E2 AF                   xor     a

 378+ A6E9              p127_physmem_cleanup_saved:

 380+ A6EC B7                   or      a
 381+ A6ED 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_cleanup_release
 382+ A6EF 3A 6C AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_saved_p0)

 384+ A6F5

 389+ A6FB CD 8F 6C             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 390+ A6FE DA 9F A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 391+ A701              p127_physmem_cleanup_resume:

 393+ A704 B7                   or      a
 394+ A705 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_cleanup_free
 395+ A707 CD A3 6C             call    p127_w3_resume
 396+ A70A DA 9F A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 397+ A70D
 398+ A70D              p127_physmem_cleanup_free:
 399+ A70D 3A 64 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 400+ A710 32 70 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 401+ A713 3A 64 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 402+ A716 5F                   ld      e,a
 403+ A717 16 00                ld      d,0
 404+ A719 21 71 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 405+ A71C 19                   add     hl,de
 406+ A71D              p127_physmem_cleanup_free_loop:

 413+ A726 CD 97 71             call    p127_mem_free
 414+ A729 E1                   pop     hl
 415+ A72A DA 9F A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 416+ A72D 3A 70 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 417+ A730 3D                   dec     a
 418+ A731 32 70 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 419+ A734 18 E7                jr      p127_physmem_cleanup_free_loop
 420+ A736              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:
 421+ A736                      ; Reallocation must return the same lowest free page and release it.
 422+ A736 3A 63 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)
 423+ A739 B7                   or      a
 424+ A73A 20 21                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return
 425+ A73C CD 81 71             call    p127_mem_alloc
 426+ A73F DA 9F A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 427+ A742 47                   ld      b,a
 428+ A743 3A 69 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)
 429+ A746 B8                   cp      b
 430+ A747 C2 9F A8             jp      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 431+ A74A 78                   ld      a,b
 432+ A74B CD 97 71             call    p127_mem_free
 433+ A74E DA 9F A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 434+ A751 CD A6 A8             call    p127_physmem_check_reserved
 435+ A754 DA 9F A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 436+ A757 CD 06 A9             call    p127_physmem_check_free_state
 437+ A75A DA 9F A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 438+ A75D              p127_physmem_cleanup_return:
 439+ A75D 3A 63 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)
 440+ A760 B7                   or      a
 441+ A761 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error
 442+ A763                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A763                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A763                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.
 445+ A763 CD 6A A7             call    p127_physmem_replay_page_log
 446+ A766 B7                   or      a
 447+ A767 C9                   ret
 448+ A768              p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error:
 449+ A768 37                   scf
 450+ A769 C9                   ret
 451+ A76A
 452+ A76A              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A76A              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A76A              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A76A                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A76A                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A76A                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A76A                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.
 459+ A76A CD 11 A0             call    p126_atm_video_init

 570+ A89F              p127_physmem_cleanup_error:

 573+ A8A4 37                   scf
 574+ A8A5 C9                   ret
 575+ A8A6
 576+ A8A6              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ A8A6              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.
 578+ A8A6              p127_physmem_check_reserved:
 579+ A8A6 21 42 72             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 580+ A8A9 06 08                ld      b,8
 581+ A8AB 0E 00                ld      c,0
 582+ A8AD              p127_physmem_reserved_loop:
 583+ A8AD 79                   ld      a,c
 584+ A8AE FE 06                cp      6
 585+ A8B0 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ A8B2 FE 07                cp      7
 587+ A8B4 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ A8B6 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ A8B8 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ A8BA FE 01                cp      1
 591+ A8BC 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ A8BE FE 02                cp      2
 593+ A8C0 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ A8C2 FE 03                cp      3
 595+ A8C4 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ A8C6 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ A8C8 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ A8CA FE 05                cp      5
 599+ A8CC 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ A8CE 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ A8D0              p127_physmem_reserved_check:
 602+ A8D0 C5                   push    bc
 603+ A8D1 E5                   push    hl
 604+ A8D2 59                   ld      e,c
 605+ A8D3 16 00                ld      d,0
 606+ A8D5 21 42 72             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 607+ A8D8 19                   add     hl,de
 608+ A8D9 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 609+ A8DA FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 610+ A8DC E1                   pop     hl
 611+ A8DD C1                   pop     bc
 612+ A8DE 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ A8E0              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:
 614+ A8E0 0C                   inc     c
 615+ A8E1 10 CA                djnz    p127_physmem_reserved_loop
 616+ A8E3 B7                   or      a
 617+ A8E4 C9                   ret
 618+ A8E5              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ A8E5 37                   scf
 620+ A8E6 C9                   ret
 621+ A8E7
 622+ A8E7              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ A8E7              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ A8E7 3A 64 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 625+ A8EA 47                   ld      b,a
 626+ A8EB 21 71 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 627+ A8EE              p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop:
 628+ A8EE 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 629+ A8EF 5F                   ld      e,a
 630+ A8F0 16 00                ld      d,0
 631+ A8F2 C5                   push    bc
 632+ A8F3 E5                   push    hl
 633+ A8F4 21 42 72             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 634+ A8F7 19                   add     hl,de
 635+ A8F8 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 636+ A8F9 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 637+ A8FB E1                   pop     hl
 638+ A8FC C1                   pop     bc
 639+ A8FD 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail
 640+ A8FF 23                   inc     hl
 641+ A900 10 EC                djnz    p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop
 642+ A902 B7                   or      a
 643+ A903 C9                   ret
 644+ A904              p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail:
 645+ A904 37                   scf
 646+ A905 C9                   ret
 647+ A906
 648+ A906              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ A906              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ A906              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ A906 21 42 72             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ A909 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ A90B 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ A90D              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ A90D 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ A90E FE 00                cp      0
 657+ A910 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ A912 FE 01                cp      1
 659+ A914 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ A916 FE 02                cp      2
 661+ A918 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ A91A FE 03                cp      3
 663+ A91C 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ A91E FE 04                cp      4
 665+ A920 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ A922 FE 05                cp      5
 667+ A924 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ A926 FE 07                cp      7
 669+ A928 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 670+ A92A 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 671+ A92B B7                   or      a
 672+ A92C 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ A92E 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ A930              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ A930 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ A931 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ A933 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ A935              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ A935 23                   inc     hl
 680+ A936 0C                   inc     c
 681+ A937 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop
 682+ A939 B7                   or      a
 683+ A93A C9                   ret
 684+ A93B              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ A93B 37                   scf
 686+ A93C C9                   ret
 687+ A93D
 688+ A93D              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ A93D              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ A93D
 691+ A93D              ; ============================================================================
 692+ A93D              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ A93D              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ A93D              ; Public ABI:
 695+ A93D              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ A93D              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ A93D              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ A93D              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ A93D              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ A93D              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ A93D              ; ============================================================================
 702+ A93D              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ A93D C3 81 71             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ A940
 705+ A940              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ A940 32 9D AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ A943 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ A946 B7                   or      a
 709+ A947 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ A94A 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ A94D 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ A94E 3A 74 7B             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ A951 B8                   cp      b
 714+ A952 CA FA A9             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ A955 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ A958 C3 97 71             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ A95B
 718+ A95B              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ A95B              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ A95B F3                   di
 721+ A95C 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ A95F B7                   or      a
 723+ A960 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ A963 3A 74 7B             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ A966 B7                   or      a
 726+ A967 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ A96A CD 5C 6C             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ A96D D8                   ret     c
 729+ A96E 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ A970 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ A973 CD 7B 6C             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ A976 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ A978 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ A97A 32 9A AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ A97D 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ A980 32 9C AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ A983 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ A985 32 9B AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ A988 32 98 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ A98B B7                   or      a
 741+ A98C C9                   ret
 742+ A98D              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ A98D CD A3 6C             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ A990 AF                   xor     a
 745+ A991 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ A994 37                   scf
 747+ A995 C9                   ret
 748+ A996
 749+ A996              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ A996              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ A996 32 9D AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ A999 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ A99C FE 01                cp      1
 754+ A99E C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ A9A1 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ A9A4 FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ A9A6 D2 FA A9             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ A9A9 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ A9AA 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ A9AC 21 42 72             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ A9AF 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ A9B0 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ A9B1 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ A9B3 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ A9B6 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ A9B9 C3 26 AB             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ A9BC
 768+ A9BC              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ A9BC              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ A9BC              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ A9BC 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ A9BF FE 01                cp      1
 773+ A9C1 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ A9C4 AF                   xor     a
 775+ A9C5 CD 26 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ A9C8 D8                   ret     c
 777+ A9C9 3A 9B AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ A9CC B7                   or      a
 779+ A9CD 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ A9CF 3A 9C AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ A9D2 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ A9D5              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ A9D5 3A 9A AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ A9D8 B7                   or      a
 785+ A9D9 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ A9DB CD 8F 6C             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ A9DE D8                   ret     c
 788+ A9DF AF                   xor     a
 789+ A9E0 32 9A AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ A9E3              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ A9E3 3A 99 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ A9E6 B7                   or      a
 793+ A9E7 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ A9E9 CD A3 6C             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ A9EC D8                   ret     c
 796+ A9ED AF                   xor     a
 797+ A9EE 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ A9F1              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ A9F1 AF                   xor     a
 800+ A9F2 32 9B AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ A9F5 32 98 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ A9F8 B7                   or      a
 803+ A9F9 C9                   ret
 804+ A9FA
 805+ A9FA              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ A9FA 37                   scf
 807+ A9FB C9                   ret
 808+ A9FC
 809+ A9FC              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ A9FC              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ A9FC              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ A9FC AF                   xor     a
 813+ A9FD 32 98 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ AA00 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ AA03 32 9A AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ AA06 32 9B AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ AA09 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ AA0B CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ AA0E D2 96 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ AA11 CD 3D A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AA14 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AA17 32 9E AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AA1A 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AA1D 32 9F AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AA20 CD 5B A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AA23 DA 90 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AA26                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AA26 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AA28 CD 96 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AA2B D2 8D AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AA2E 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AA31 CD 96 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AA34 DA 8D AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AA37                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AA37 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AA3A CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AA3D D2 8D AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AA40 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AA42 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AA45 CD BC A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AA48 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AA4B 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AA4E 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AA4F 3A 9F AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AA52 B8                   cp      b
 846+ AA53 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AA56 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AA59 CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AA5C DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AA5F                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AA5F CD 3D A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AA62 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AA65 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AA66 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AA69 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AA6A C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AA6D 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AA6E CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AA71 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AA74 3A C9 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AA77 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AA79 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AA7C 3A CA 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AA7F FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AA81 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AA84 3A 74 7B             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AA87 B7                   or      a
 868+ AA88 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AA8B B7                   or      a
 870+ AA8C C9                   ret
 871+ AA8D
 872+ AA8D              p127_physmem_api_test_end:
 873+ AA8D CD BC A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 874+ AA90              p127_physmem_api_test_free:
 875+ AA90 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 876+ AA93 CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 877+ AA96              p127_physmem_api_test_fail:
 878+ AA96 37                   scf
 879+ AA97 C9                   ret
 880+ AA98
 881+ AA98 00           p127_physmem_api_active: db 0
 882+ AA99 00           p127_physmem_api_quiesced: db 0

 890+ AAA0              p127_physmem_trace:
 891+ AAA0 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 892+ AAA3 ED 79                out     (c),a
 893+ AAA5 C9                   ret
 894+ AAA6
 895+ AAA6              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ AAA6              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ AAA6              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ AAA6              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ AAA6 F5                   push    af
 900+ AAA7 C5                   push    bc
 901+ AAA8 D5                   push    de
 902+ AAA9 E5                   push    hl
 903+ AAAA FE 46                cp      'F'
 904+ AAAC 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_trace_page_verify
 905+ AAAE 21 F8 AA             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_fill_text
 906+ AAB1 18 03                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 907+ AAB3              p127_physmem_trace_page_verify:
 908+ AAB3 21 06 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_verify_text
 909+ AAB6              p127_physmem_trace_page_text:
 910+ AAB6 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 911+ AAB7 B7                   or      a
 912+ AAB8 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_trace_page_value
 913+ AABA 23                   inc     hl
 914+ AABB E5                   push    hl
 915+ AABC CD F2 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 916+ AABF E1                   pop     hl
 917+ AAC0 18 F4                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 918+ AAC2              p127_physmem_trace_page_value:
 919+ AAC2 3A 6F AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 920+ AAC5 CD D7 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_hex8
 921+ AAC8 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 922+ AACA CD F2 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 923+ AACD 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 924+ AACF CD F2 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 925+ AAD2 E1                   pop     hl
 926+ AAD3 D1                   pop     de
 927+ AAD4 C1                   pop     bc
 928+ AAD5 F1                   pop     af
 929+ AAD6 C9                   ret
 930+ AAD7
 931+ AAD7              p127_physmem_trace_hex8:
 932+ AAD7 F5                   push    af
 933+ AAD8 E6 F0                and     0F0h
 934+ AADA 0F                   rrca
 935+ AADB 0F                   rrca
 936+ AADC 0F                   rrca
 937+ AADD 0F                   rrca
 938+ AADE CD E7 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 939+ AAE1 F1                   pop     af
 940+ AAE2 E6 0F                and     00Fh
 941+ AAE4 C3 E7 AA             jp      p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 942+ AAE7
 943+ AAE7              p127_physmem_trace_nibble:
 944+ AAE7 5F                   ld      e,a
 945+ AAE8 16 00                ld      d,0
 946+ AAEA 21 16 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_hex_table
 947+ AAED 19                   add     hl,de
 948+ AAEE 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 949+ AAEF C3 F2 AA             jp      p127_physmem_trace_char
 950+ AAF2
 951+ AAF2              p127_physmem_trace_char:
 952+ AAF2 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 953+ AAF5 ED 79                out     (c),a
 954+ AAF7 C9                   ret
 955+ AAF8
 956+ AAF8              p127_physmem_trace_fill_text:
 957+ AAF8 46 49 4C 4C          db      "FILL PAGE ID=",0
 957+ AAFC 20 50 41 47
 957+ AB00 45 20 49 44
 957+ AB04 3D 00
 958+ AB06              p127_physmem_trace_verify_text:
 959+ AB06 56 45 52 49          db      "VERIFY PAGE ID=",0
 959+ AB0A 46 59 20 50
 959+ AB0E 41 47 45 20
 959+ AB12 49 44 3D 00
 960+ AB16              p127_physmem_trace_hex_table:
 961+ AB16 30 31 32 33          db      "0123456789ABCDEF"
 961+ AB1A 34 35 36 37
 961+ AB1E 38 39 41 42
 961+ AB22 43 44 45 46
 962+ AB26
 963+ AB26              ; IN A=physical RAM page ID. Program only W3 (#FFF7), never W0/W1/W2.
 964+ AB26              ; D6=1 selects RAM, D7=0 selects the inverse D0-D5 page ID.
 965+ AB26              p127_physmem_set_w3:
 966+ AB26 32 6B AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_arg),a
 967+ AB29 CD 91 79             call    p127_map_encode_page
 968+ AB2C D8                   ret     c
 969+ AB2D 01 F7 FF             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT
 970+ AB30 ED 79                out     (c),a
 971+ AB32 3A 6B AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_arg)
 972+ AB35 32 74 7B             ld      (p127_map_shadow+3),a
 973+ AB38 B7                   or      a
 974+ AB39 C9                   ret
 975+ AB3A
 976+ AB3A              ; IN D=pattern seed. Byte(address)=ROL(address_low) XOR address_high XOR D.
 977+ AB3A              ; The same address produces a different expected value at each seed.
 978+ AB3A              p127_physmem_fill_pattern:
 979+ AB3A 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 980+ AB3D 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 981+ AB40              .p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop:
 982+ AB40 7D                   ld      a,l
 983+ AB41 07                   rlca
 984+ AB42 AC                   xor     h
 985+ AB43 AA                   xor     d
 986+ AB44 77                   ld      (hl),a
 987+ AB45 23                   inc     hl
 988+ AB46 0B                   dec     bc
 989+ AB47 78                   ld      a,b
 990+ AB48 B1                   or      c
 991+ AB49 20 F5                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop
 992+ AB4B C9                   ret
 993+ AB4C
 994+ AB4C              ; IN D=pattern seed. Verify all 16 KiB against the address-derived pattern.
 995+ AB4C              p127_physmem_verify_pattern:
 996+ AB4C 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 997+ AB4F 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 998+ AB52              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop:
 999+ AB52 7D                   ld      a,l
1000+ AB53 07                   rlca
1001+ AB54 AC                   xor     h
1002+ AB55 AA                   xor     d
1003+ AB56 BE                   cp      (hl)
1004+ AB57 20 08                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail
1005+ AB59 23                   inc     hl
1006+ AB5A 0B                   dec     bc
1007+ AB5B 78                   ld      a,b
1008+ AB5C B1                   or      c
1009+ AB5D 20 F3                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop
1010+ AB5F B7                   or      a
1011+ AB60 C9                   ret
1012+ AB61              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail:
1013+ AB61 37                   scf
1014+ AB62 C9                   ret
